Distinct from /cs-ops:cold-start-interview which is the initial setup flow for a blank configuration. - **customize**: View and update your onboarding plugin configuration — the profile that drives milestone targets, TtV benchmarks, success criteria format, escalation contacts, graduation criteria, and CS methodology references. Use --view (default) to display current configuration with section-by-section status, --update to change specific settings by section name, --reset to restore a section to its template defaults, or --validate to check the configuration for missing required fields, placeholder values, and internal consistency issues before running onboarding skills. - **customize**: Configure the CSM plugin for your company — CS motion, health model, churn signal definitions, escalation matrix, playbook, and tool integrations.
